# what each operand rule of Arm's instruction model is as an operand, one line per rule an instruction uses
#
# the instruction model (AARCHMRS) states each rule's text and the fields it is encoded in and redacts what the text
# means, so every fact here is read from the instruction's page of the A64 ISA XML 2026-09, cited by its file and
# symbol, and no line overrides the model. an expression is the page's own: decimal numbers, `UInt(...)` and
# `SInt(...)` of fields, field slices `F[hi:lo]` and quoted bits joined by `:`, and `+`, `-` and `*`
#
#   register <rule> | class=<classes> [role=<role>] [number="<expr>"] [step=<n>] [default=<register>]
#                     [by=<fields> <bits>="<expr>" ...]
#     a register of the first class, or of the first that has its number where the text is the choice of a class's
#     one name and the numbered text, as `(XZR|X{UInteger})`. a class `*` is the one the rows member written right
#     before it names, as `<V><d>` is s5 where V is `S`, and `*zr` that class's zero register. `number` is its number
#     where its fields' bits alone are not, `step` the stride of the numbers it admits, and `by` its number by the
#     bits of other fields, one expression per pattern of them. `default` is the register it is where its text is
#     left out. a `role` is every instruction's using the rule, which is why gen refuses a role line naming the rule, and
#     a rule whose instructions differ states none and takes a role line from each of them
#   immediate <rule> | <unsigned|signed|either|float8|bitmask|bytemask> [value="<expr>"]
#                      [by=<fields> <bits>="<expr>" ...] [range=<lo>,<hi>] [default=<n>] [unless=<symbol>:<text>]
#                      [derived=<bits>]
#     a number of its fields' bits, `value` where they do not hold it whole, `range` the values it admits where the bits
#     hold more, and `default` its value where its text is left out. a float8 is the 8-bit floating-point immediate, a
#     bitmask the logical immediate of N:immr:imms, and a bytemask movi's 64-bit immediate, a byte of ones or zeros per
#     bit of a:b:c:d:e:f:g:h. `unless` keeps the text from being left out where the operand the page writes as
#     `<symbol>` is the member `<text>`, as `<amount>` required when `<extend>` is LSL. `derived` is an alias's number
#     of `<bits>` bits that the derivation the aliases topic names computes from its fields, as lsl's `<shift>` from
#     immr and imms, which holds no bits of its own, and `either` a number of its bits read signed or unsigned, as mov's
#     32-bit `#-1` or `#0xffffffff`
#   relative <rule> | value="<expr>" [page=yes]
#     a program label, the expression its offset from the instruction, or from its 4KB page under `page`
#   choice <rule> | <rows|enumerant|mask> [default=<text>] [fields=<field>] [unless=<symbol>:<text>]
#                   [where=<text>:<rule>[,<rule>]=<n>] <text>=<bits>[,<bits>] ...
#     a choice of texts, each member by the text it spells and the bits of its fields it encodes from the high bit,
#     `x` a bit it leaves, `:` between fields, one pattern per cube of a member of several. `rows` makes a row of each
#     member, an `enumerant` is one operand of the members' values, and a `mask` one of any union of them. the empty
#     text is the bits an optional text encodes when left out, `#` a value spelled by its number, and `default` the
#     member an enumerant takes where its text is left out, and `@<features>` after a member's bits the features it
#     needs beyond its instruction's, as its page's value table states them, alternatives `/` of features `+` all held,
#     as dc's `CGDSW=000:1010:110@FEAT_MTE2`. `unless` is an immediate's. `where` spells the member
#     `<text>`, and takes it where the text is left out, only where the register of one of the named rules is number
#     `<n>`, as add's LSL where `<Xd|SP>` or `<Xn|SP>` is sp, and every other spelling of its bits names another
#     member. a choice of branches by `fields`, as `<Wm>` or `<Xm>` by option, names each branch's rule as a member
#   choice <rule> | one=<rule>
#     a choice of the named operand of its one rule beside its spelling by number, as `<prfop>` or `#<imm5>`: one
#     operand, every value of its bits admitted, the ones no member names spelled by number
#   element <specifier> | bits=<n> [lanes=<n>]
#     the width of each element of a register written `.<specifier>`, and how many it holds
#   role <instruction> | <rule>=<role> ...
#     the role of a register of one instruction, for each rule of it whose line states no role. gen refuses a line
#     for a rule that states one, a rule whose lines all state one role, and a line no row of the instruction reads
#   fixed <instruction> | <field>=<bits> ...
#     a field whose every other value the instruction's decode refuses where no operand of a row holds it, as MOPS's
#     sz, or fcmla's L where a 32-bit element's index is H alone
#   ignored <instruction> | <field> ...
#     a field whose bits no operand of a row holds the decode ignores, which an assembler writes zero, as dup's imm5
#     above the bit its element size sets
#   reserved <instruction> | <field>=<bits> ...
#     bits of a field the instruction's decode refuses, or makes CONSTRAINED UNPREDICTABLE with no outcome but UNDEF
#     or NOP, which the one operand holding the field never takes, as LSE128's Rt 11111, each a value of its own. a
#     field may join several, as `size:sh`, where a row whose fixed bits meet the pattern refuses the rest of it
#   apart <instruction> | <rule>=<rule>[,<rule>...] ...
#     a register the instruction keeps apart from the others named, where its pages make them one register
#     CONSTRAINED UNPREDICTABLE, as ldp's <Xt2> from <Xt1> and under writeback its base from both
#   needs <instruction> | <feature> <field>=<bits> ...
#     a feature the instruction's decode needs beyond its encoding's conditions where the row's fields hold the bits,
#     as pmull's FEAT_PMULL where size is 11, which the row's requirement then holds
#   withheld <instruction> | <reason>
#     an encoding of the model whose operands no page states, which no row is made of, or an alias of one, keyed
#     `<instruction>/<alias>`, which no row respells, or `<row>/<alias>`, which that row does not
#
# a role is read, write, read-write or none, read from the page's decode and execute pseudocode: the register
# accessors X, W, SP, V, Z, P, FFR, ZA and ZT0 by the decode variable of the register's fields, with the branches of
# constant booleans and of the fields the encoding fixes taken. a register read or written only under a state the
# encoding does not fix, as a merging destination's old elements or a write-back that depends on the implementation, is
# read or written, and the shared functions a page calls state their registers' roles where it names them
